\line{\hskip-4cm\hbox to 14.7cm{\reg\hfill per quattuor hebdomadas distributum\hfill}\hss}\vskip.6cm%
\vskip-.6cm
\vskip.4cm\vbox{\leftskip-3cm\rightskip.65cm\noindent\ita%
Ubicumque psalmus in plures partes divisus est, licet psalmum integrum sine interruptione 
persolvere, adhibita prima tantum antiphona\/\ {\reg(}cf.\/ {\reg Institutio ge\-ne\-ra\-lis de Liturgia Horarum, 115)}. 
Antiphonæ ad Inv. pro Hebdomada II, III, IV sumuntur ex Hebdomada I.
}\vskip.4cm%
\goodbreak\line{\hskip-4.6cm\hbox to 14.7cm{\bmed\hfill HEBDOMADA I\hfill}\hss}\nobreak\vskip.6cm%
